```blade
        <form wire:submit="save" id="change-status-form" class="flex flex-col gap-4">
            @if ($mode === 'status')
                <x-ui.field>
                    <x-ui.field-label for="cs-status">{{ __('New status') }}</x-ui.field-label>
                    <x-ui.select native id="cs-status" wire:model.live="statusId" class="h-11 text-base md:h-9 md:text-sm">
                        <option value="">{{ __('Choose…') }}</option>
                        @foreach ($statuses as $status)
                            <option value="{{ $status->id }}">{{ $status->name }}</option>
                        @endforeach
                    </x-ui.select>
                    <x-ui.field-error :messages="$errors->get('lead_status_id')" />
                </x-ui.field>

                @if ($this->needsFollowUp)
                    <x-ui.alert>

                    </x-ui.alert>
                    <div class="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The quick-log sheet**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Activities/QuickLog.php` follows `ChangeStatus`:

- Locked `subjectType`, `subjectId`, `mode`, `activityId`.
- Fields: `activity_type_id`, `title`, `description`, `done` (bool), `scheduled_at` (string, `Y-m-d\TH:i`), `duration_minutes`, `outcome_id`, `owner_user_id`, `reminder_minutes` (default `(string) Settings::get('crm.reminder_lead_minutes', 30)` when it is one of the options, else `''`), `location_text`, `scheduleNext` (bool), `next_type_id`, `next_at`.
- `#[On('crm-log-activity')] open(string $subjectType, int $subjectId, string $mode = 'log', ?int $activity = null)`:
  - Reset fields.
  - Resolve the subject with `Relation::getMorphedModel($subjectType)` (only `lead` and `customer` are accepted; anything else 404s), then `findOrFail`, then `authorize('view', $subject)`.
  - `done` = `$mode === 'log'`.
  - For `complete` and `reschedule`, load the activity. It must belong to that subject (`subject_type`/`subject_id` match, else 404). Prefill `scheduled_at` from it.
  - Dispatch `open-sheet-quick-log`.
- `updatedActivityTypeId()` sets `title` to the type name when `title` is empty or still equals the previous type's name.
- `save(LogActivity, CompleteActivity, RescheduleActivity)` re-resolves subject and activity and calls the matching Action:
  - `log` / `schedule` → `LogActivity` with `done`, `next_follow_up` only when `scheduleNext`.
  - `complete` → `CompleteActivity` with outcome, description, duration and next follow-up.
  - `reschedule` → `RescheduleActivity`.
  - Then dispatch `close-sheet-quick-log`, `crm-activity-saved` and a toast.
- `render()` passes active outcomes, the reminder options (`LogActivity::REMINDER_OPTIONS` with labels "15 min", "30 min", "1 h", "1 day") and owners (active users who `can('crm.activities.view')`).

`resources/views/livewire/crm/activities/quick-log.blade.php` — `x-shell.sheet id="quick-log"`, title by mode ("Log activity", "Schedule follow-up", "Mark done", "Reschedule"):
- `log`/`schedule`: a `x-ui.segmented-control` "Done now / Scheduled" bound to `done` (hidden in `complete`/`reschedule`); Type* (`x-lookup-select table="activity_types"`, `wire:model.live`), Title, Description.
- Scheduled: When* (`datetime-local`), Reminder (native select: None + options), Owner (native select).
- Done: Duration (minutes, `inputmode="numeric"`; label shows "required for this type" when the type requires it), Outcome (`x-lookup-select table="activity_outcomes"`), Location (for meeting / site visit types — show when the chosen type's code is `MEETING`, `SITE_VISIT` or `OFFICE_VISIT`).
- Done or complete: a "Schedule next follow-up" switch revealing Type and When.
- `reschedule`: only When and Reminder.
- Footer: Cancel and Save, like the change-status sheet.

`resources/views/livewire/crm/activities/partials/timeline.blade.php` — receives `$activities` (collection, `type`, `outcome`, `owner` loaded), `$subjectType`, `$subjectId` and `$showSubject` (bool):
- An `x-ui.item-group`; each activity is an `x-ui.item variant="outline"`:
  - `x-ui.item-media` with the type icon (`<x-dynamic-component :component="'lucide-'.($activity->type->icon ?: 'circle')" />`);
  - title, then description (line-clamped);
  - meta line: scheduled or completed time (`d-M-Y h:i A`), owner, outcome badge, an "Overdue" destructive badge when `isOverdue()`, and the subject link when `$showSubject`.
- Open activities have actions:
  - desktop: icon buttons "Mark done" (dispatches `crm-log-activity` with `mode: 'complete'`) and "Reschedule";
  - mobile: one 44 px button dispatching the same events.
  - Shown only with `crm.activities.update`. Delete (with `crm.activities.delete`) calls `deleteActivity(id)` on the host component, behind a confirmation sheet `activity-delete`.
- Open activities first (by `scheduled_at`), then done ones (latest `completed_at` first). The host passes them already ordered.

- [ ] **Step 5: The detail page**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Leads/Show.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Livewire\Leads;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Crm\Actions\AssignLead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Actions\DeleteActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Actions\DeleteLead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\CrmActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use Illuminate\Contracts\View\View;
use Livewire\Attributes\On;
use Livewire\Attributes\Url;
use Livewire\Component;

/**
 * Lead detail (docs/03 §5.3): header actions, pipeline bar, tabs and the summary rail.
 */
class Show extends Component
{
    public Lead $lead;

    #[Url(except: 'overview')]
    public string $tab = 'overview';

    public string $assignTo = '';

    public string $assignReason = '';

    public ?int $deletingActivityId = null;

    public function mount(Lead $lead): void
    {
        $this->authorize('view', $lead);
        $this->lead = $lead;
    }

    #[On('crm-lead-updated')]
    #[On('crm-activity-saved')]
    public function refreshLead(): void
    {
        $this->lead->refresh();
    }

    public function assign(AssignLead $assignLead): void
    {
        $this->authorize('assign', $this->lead);

        $assignLead->handle($this->actor(), $this->lead, $this->assignTo === '' ? null : (int) $this->assignTo, $this->assignReason ?: null);

        $this->reset('assignTo', 'assignReason');
        $this->lead->refresh();
        $this->dispatch('close-sheet-lead-assign');
        $this->dispatch('toast', type: 'success', description: __('Lead assigned.'));
    }

    public function confirmDeleteActivity(int $id): void
    {
        $this->deletingActivityId = $this->lead->activities()->findOrFail($id)->id;
        $this->dispatch('open-sheet-activity-delete');
    }

    public function deleteActivity(DeleteActivity $deleteActivity): void
    {
        $activity = $this->lead->activities()->findOrFail($this->deletingActivityId);

        $deleteActivity->handle($this->actor(), $activity);

        $this->deletingActivityId = null;
        $this->lead->refresh();
        $this->dispatch('close-sheet-activity-delete');
    }

    public function deleteLead(DeleteLead $deleteLead): void
    {
        $deleteLead->handle($this->actor(), $this->lead);

        session()->flash('success', __('Lead deleted.'));
        $this->redirectRoute('crm.leads.index', navigate: true);
    }

    private function actor(): User
    {
        /** @var User */
        return auth()->user();
    }

    public function render(AssignLead $assignLead): View
    {
        $this->lead->load([
            'status', 'source', 'priority', 'level', 'businessLine', 'location', 'assignee', 'team', 'lostReason',
            'convertedCustomer', 'referrerCustomer', 'referrerUser', 'services.service',
        ]);

        $activities = $this->lead->activities()->with(['type', 'outcome', 'owner:id,name'])
            ->orderByRaw('completed_at IS NOT NULL')
            ->orderBy('scheduled_at')
            ->orderByDesc('completed_at')
            ->get();

        $enteredStatusAt = $this->lead->statusHistories()->where('to_status_id', $this->lead->lead_status_id)->max('changed_at');

        return view('livewire.crm.leads.show', [
            'activities' => $activities,
            'statuses' => \App\Modules\Crm\Models\LeadStatus::query()->active()->open()->ordered()->get(['id', 'name', 'code']),
            'ageDays' => (int) $this->lead->lead_date->diffInDays(today()),
            'daysInStatus' => $enteredStatusAt ? (int) \Illuminate\Support\Carbon::parse($enteredStatusAt)->diffInDays(now()) : null,
            'assignees' => $this->actor()->can('assign', $this->lead) ? $assignLead->assignableUsers($this->actor()) : collect(),
            'statusHistory' => $this->lead->statusHistories()->with(['fromStatus:id,name', 'toStatus:id,name', 'changer:id,name'])->get(),
            'assignmentHistory' => $this->lead->assignmentHistories()->with(['fromUser:id,name', 'toUser:id,name', 'assigner:id,name'])->get(),
        ])
            ->title($this->lead->lead_number)
            ->layoutData(['back' => url()->previous()]);
    }
}
```

(Import `LeadStatus` and `Carbon` with `use` lines.) The `back` link uses `url()->previous()` until Task 12 adds the list route and switches it to `route('crm.leads.index')`.

`resources/views/livewire/crm/leads/show.blade.php`:
- **Desktop header** (`hidden md:flex`): number (mono, muted), name (`text-2xl`), status badge (status color), priority badge, owner (assignee name or "Unassigned").
  - Actions, each gated by `@can`: Edit (`crm.leads.edit`, only when `can('update', $lead)`), Log activity (`$dispatch('crm-log-activity', {subjectType: 'lead', subjectId: {{ $lead->id }}, mode: 'log'})`), Schedule follow-up (`mode: 'schedule'`), Change status (`$dispatch('crm-change-status', {lead: '{{ $lead->lead_number }}', mode: 'status'})`), Assign (opens `lead-assign`), Mark lost (`mode: 'lost'`), Reopen (only when LOST), Print (`crm.leads.print`, `target="_blank"`), Delete (destructive, behind a `lead-delete` confirmation sheet).
  - Primary actions show as buttons; the rest go in an `x-ui.dropdown-menu`.
- **Pipeline bar** (open leads only): the open `$statuses` as a row of steps, the current one highlighted. Clicking a step dispatches `crm-change-status` with that status. On mobile it is a horizontally scrollable chip row.
- **Converted banner** when converted: an `x-ui.alert` "Converted to customer {name} on {date}" with a link (customer show arrives in Task 14; until then link to `crm.customers.edit` when the user can update it, otherwise plain text).
- **Tabs** (`wire:model.live="tab"`): Overview · Activities · Documents · Notes · History. Desktop uses `x-ui.tabs` styled triggers bound to `tab` (`wire:click="$set('tab', '…')"`); mobile uses `x-ui.segmented-control` (scrollable).
  - **Overview:** an `x-ui.description-list` of Contact (phone with `tel:` link, WhatsApp with `https://wa.me/88…` link, office phone, email, address, location `full_path`), Enquiry (date, source, referrer label, business line, level, priority, expected value, expected close, site location, land area, floors) and the services table (service, estimated value, notes). Mobile: one stacked column.
  - **Activities:** the timeline partial plus a "Log activity" button.
  - **Documents:** `<livewire:foundation.attachments :model="$lead" :key="'att-'.$lead->id" />`.
  - **Notes:** `<livewire:foundation.notes :model="$lead" :key="'notes-'.$lead->id" />`.
  - **History:** status history list (from → to, by, when, note), assignment history (from → to, by, when, reason), then `<livewire:foundation.history :model="$lead" ... />`.
- **Right rail** (desktop `lg:` third column): cards for Age (days), Days in status, Last activity, Next follow-up (red when overdue), Expected value (`Money::format`), Converted customer. **Mobile:** these become summary cards in a 2-column grid under the header (allowed: they are summary stats, not a content grid).
- **Mobile top bar:** title = lead number; one top-bar action button (`x-slot:actions` of the layout, if the layout exposes it — it does: `layouts/app.blade.php` forwards `actions`) opening a bottom sheet `lead-actions` that lists the same actions as 44 px rows.
- **Sheets on the page:** `lead-assign` (assignee native select of `$assignees`, reason input, Save → `assign`), `lead-delete` (confirm → `deleteLead`), `activity-delete` (confirm → `deleteActivity`), plus `<livewire:crm.change-status />` and `<livewire:crm.quick-log />`.

Register the Livewire aliases in `CrmServiceProvider::boot()`:

```php
        Livewire::component('crm.change-status', ChangeStatus::class);
        Livewire::component('crm.quick-log', QuickLog::class);
```

`resources/views/crm/leads/print.blade.php`: a standalone A4 page (`<!DOCTYPE html>`, `@vite(['resources/css/app.css'])`, `<body class="bg-white text-sm">`):

```php
    Route::get('leads/{lead:lead_number}/print', fn (Lead $lead) => view('crm.leads.print', [
        'lead' => $lead->load(['status', 'source', 'priority', 'level', 'businessLine', 'location', 'assignee', 'services.service', 'activities.type', 'activities.outcome', 'activities.owner']),

    ]))->middleware('can:view,lead')->name('leads.print');
    Route::livewire('leads/{lead:lead_number}', Leads\Show::class)->middleware('can:view,lead')->name('leads.show');
```

Route order: Task 12 adds `leads` and Task 13 adds `leads/create` — both must be registered **before** `leads/{lead:lead_number}` so `create` is not read as a lead number. Keep the show route last in the leads block.

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Catalog\Models\Service;
use App\Modules\Crm\Livewire\Leads\Index;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\CrmActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;

use Livewire\Livewire;

beforeEach(function () {
    seedCrm();
    Notification::fake();
    $this->me = userWithPermissions('crm.leads.view_own', 'crm.leads.update', 'crm.activities.view_own', 'crm.activities.create');
    $this->mine = Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->me)->create(['name' => 'My Lead', 'phone' => '01711000111', 'expected_value' => '100000']);
    $this->theirs = Lead::factory()->assignedTo(User::factory()->create())->create(['name' => 'Their Lead']);
});

test('the list needs a lead view permission and shows only visible leads (CRM-AC-02)', function () {
    $this->actingAs(userWithPermissions('crm.customers.view_all'));
    $this->get(route('crm.leads.index'))->assertForbidden();

    $this->actingAs($this->me);
    $this->get(route('crm.leads.index'))->assertOk()->assertSee('My Lead')->assertDontSee('Their Lead');
});

test('search covers number, name and phone; filters by service and status', function () {
    $service = Service::factory()->create();
    $this->mine->services()->create(['service_id' => $service->id]);
    Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->me)->withStatus('CONTACTED')->create(['name' => 'Contacted Lead']);

    $component = Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class);

    $component->set('search', '01711000111')->assertSee('My Lead')->assertDontSee('Contacted Lead');
    $component->set('search', '')->set('filters.service', (string) $service->id)->assertSee('My Lead')->assertDontSee('Contacted Lead');
    $component->set('filters', [])->set('statusFilter', [(string) LeadStatus::idFor('CONTACTED')])->assertSee('Contacted Lead')->assertDontSee('My Lead');
});

test('closed leads are hidden by default and presets switch the filters', function () {
    Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->me)->withStatus('WON')->create(['name' => 'Won Lead', 'won_at' => now()]);
    Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->me)->create(['name' => 'Due Lead', 'next_follow_up_at' => now()->subHour()]);

    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->assertDontSee('Won Lead')
        ->call('applyPreset', 'won_month')->assertSee('Won Lead')->assertDontSee('My Lead')
        ->call('applyPreset', 'overdue')->assertSee('Due Lead')->assertDontSee('My Lead');
});

test('stale leads are flagged and filterable', function () {
    $this->travel(20)->days();
    Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->me)->create(['name' => 'Fresh Lead', 'last_activity_at' => now()]);

    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->set('filters.stale', '1')
        ->assertSee('My Lead')->assertDontSee('Fresh Lead');
});

test('the kanban shows open statuses with counts and value totals', function () {
    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->set('view', 'kanban')
        ->assertViewHas('columns', function ($columns) {
            $new = collect($columns)->firstWhere('code', 'NEW');

            return collect($columns)->pluck('code')->doesntContain('WON')
                && $new['count'] === 1
                && $new['total'] === '100000.00';
        });
});

test('dragging to a status that needs a follow-up opens the change-status sheet instead (CRM-AC-03)', function () {
    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->call('moveLead', (string) $this->mine->id, 0, (string) LeadStatus::idFor('MEETING'))
        ->assertDispatched('crm-change-status', lead: $this->mine->lead_number, mode: 'status', status: LeadStatus::idFor('MEETING'));

    expect($this->mine->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('NEW'));

    CrmActivity::factory()->on($this->mine)->create(['owner_user_id' => $this->me->id]);

    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)->call('moveLead', (string) $this->mine->id, 0, (string) LeadStatus::idFor('MEETING'));

    expect($this->mine->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('MEETING'));
});

test('dragging to WON or onto a hidden lead does nothing', function () {
    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->call('moveLead', (string) $this->mine->id, 0, (string) LeadStatus::idFor('WON'))
        ->assertDispatched('toast')
        ->call('moveLead', (string) $this->theirs->id, 0, (string) LeadStatus::idFor('CONTACTED'));

    expect($this->mine->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('NEW'))
        ->and($this->theirs->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('NEW'));
});

test('bulk actions skip leads outside the user scope', function () {
    Settings::set('crm.follow_up_required_on_status', []);

    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)
        ->set('selected', [(string) $this->mine->id, (string) $this->theirs->id])
        ->set('bulkStatusId', (string) LeadStatus::idFor('CONTACTED'))
        ->call('bulkChangeStatus')
        ->assertDispatched('toast');

    expect($this->mine->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('CONTACTED'))
        ->and($this->theirs->fresh()->lead_status_id)->toBe(LeadStatus::idFor('NEW'));
});

test('export needs crm.leads.export', function () {
    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)->call('export')->assertForbidden();

    $this->me->syncDirectPermissions(['crm.leads.view_own', 'crm.leads.export']);
    Livewire::actingAs($this->me)->test(Index::class)->call('export')->assertFileDownloaded();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The component**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Leads/Index.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Livewire\Leads;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Crm\Actions\AssignLead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Actions\ChangeLeadStatus;
use App\Modules\Crm\Concerns\FiltersByLocation;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\LeadStatus;
use App\Support\Exports\ListingExport;
use App\Support\Facades\Settings;
use App\Support\Listing\WithListing;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Contracts\View\View;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Livewire\Attributes\On;
use Livewire\Attributes\Title;
use Livewire\Attributes\Url;
use Livewire\Component;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\BinaryFileResponse;

#[Title('Leads')]
class Index extends Component
{
    use FiltersByLocation, WithListing;

    public const PRESETS = ['my_open', 'today', 'overdue', 'unassigned', 'won_month', 'lost_month'];

    public const KANBAN_CARDS_PER_COLUMN = 50;

    #[Url(except: 'list')]
    public string $view = 'list';

    /** @var list<string> */
    #[Url(except: [])]
    public array $statusFilter = [];

    /** @var list<string> */
    public array $selected = [];

    public string $bulkAssigneeId = '';

    public string $bulkStatusId = '';

    public function mount(): void
    {
        $this->authorize('viewAny', Lead::class);

        $this->filters['state'] ??= 'open';
    }

    public function applyPreset(string $preset): void
    {
        if (! in_array($preset, self::PRESETS, true)) {
            return;
        }

        $this->statusFilter = [];
        $this->filters = match ($preset) {
            'my_open' => ['state' => 'open', 'assigned' => 'me'],
            'today' => ['state' => 'open', 'follow_up' => 'today'],
            'overdue' => ['state' => 'open', 'follow_up' => 'overdue'],
            'unassigned' => ['state' => 'open', 'assigned' => 'none'],
            'won_month' => ['state' => 'won', 'closed_month' => '1'],
            'lost_month' => ['state' => 'lost', 'closed_month' => '1'],
        };
        $this->updatedFilters();
    }

    public function updatedStatusFilter(): void
    {
        $this->updatedFilters();
    }

    /**
     * @return Builder<Lead>
     */
    protected function listingQuery(): Builder
    {
        return Lead::query()
            ->visibleTo($this->actor(), 'crm.leads')
            ->with(['status:id,name,color,code', 'source:id,name', 'priority:id,name,color', 'level:id,name', 'businessLine:id,name', 'assignee:id,name', 'team:id,name', 'services.service:id,name'])
            ->latest('lead_date')
            ->orderByDesc('leads.id');
    }

    protected function searchColumns(): array
    {
        return ['leads.lead_number', 'leads.name', 'leads.company_name', 'leads.phone', 'leads.whatsapp', 'leads.email'];
    }

    protected function sortColumns(): array
    {
        return ['number' => 'leads.lead_number', 'date' => 'leads.lead_date', 'name' => 'leads.name', 'value' => 'leads.expected_value', 'follow_up' => 'leads.next_follow_up_at', 'activity' => 'leads.last_activity_at'];
    }

    /**
     * @param  Builder<*>  $query
     */
    protected function applyFilters(Builder $query): void
    {
        match ($this->filterString('state')) {
            'won' => $query->whereIn('leads.lead_status_id', LeadStatus::query()->select('id')->where('is_won', true)),
            'lost' => $query->whereIn('leads.lead_status_id', LeadStatus::query()->select('id')->where('is_lost', true)),
            'all' => null,
            default => $query->open(),
        };

        if ($this->filterString('closed_month') === '1') {
            $column = $this->filterString('state') === 'lost' ? 'leads.lost_at' : 'leads.won_at';
            $query->whereBetween($column, [now()->startOfMonth(), now()->endOfMonth()]);
        }

        $statusIds = array_values(array_filter(array_map('intval', array_filter($this->statusFilter, 'is_string'))));

        if ($statusIds !== []) {
            $query->whereIn('leads.lead_status_id', $statusIds);
        }

        foreach (['source' => 'lead_source_id', 'business_line' => 'business_line_id', 'level' => 'lead_level_id', 'priority' => 'lead_priority_id', 'team' => 'sales_team_id'] as $key => $column) {
            if ($this->filterString($key) !== '') {
                $query->where('leads.'.$column, (int) $this->filterString($key));
            }
        }

        if ($this->filterString('service') !== '') {
            $query->whereHas('services', fn (Builder $query) => $query->where('service_id', (int) $this->filterString('service')));
        }

        match ($this->filterString('assigned')) {
            '' => null,
            'me' => $query->where('leads.assigned_to', $this->actor()->id),
            'none' => $query->whereNull('leads.assigned_to'),
            default => $query->where('leads.assigned_to', (int) $this->filterString('assigned')),
        };

        if ($this->filterString('location') !== '') {
            $this->whereInLocation($query, 'leads.location_id', (int) $this->filterString('location'));
        }

        if ($from = $this->validDate($this->filters['date_from'] ?? null)) {
            $query->whereDate('leads.lead_date', '>=', $from);
        }

        if ($to = $this->validDate($this->filters['date_to'] ?? null)) {
            $query->whereDate('leads.lead_date', '<=', $to);
        }

        match ($this->filterString('follow_up')) {
            'today' => $query->whereBetween('leads.next_follow_up_at', [today()->startOfDay(), today()->endOfDay()]),
            'overdue' => $query->where('leads.next_follow_up_at', '<', now()),
            'none' => $query->whereNull('leads.next_follow_up_at'),
            default => null,
        };

        if ($this->filterString('stale') === '1') {
            $query->stale($this->staleDays());
        }

        match ($this->filterString('converted')) {
            '1' => $query->whereNotNull('leads.converted_customer_id'),
            '0' => $query->whereNull('leads.converted_customer_id'),
            default => null,
        };
    }

    /**
     * Kanban columns: open statuses in order, each with its cards, count and Σ expected value
     * (docs/03 §5.1). The status filter is ignored here; every other filter applies.
     *
     * @return list<array{id: int, code: string, name: string, color: ?string, count: int, total: string, leads: \Illuminate\Support\Collection<int, Lead>}>
     */
    public function kanbanColumns(): array
    {
        [$statusFilter, $filters] = [$this->statusFilter, $this->filters];
        $this->statusFilter = [];
        $this->filters = [...$filters, 'state' => 'open', 'closed_month' => ''];

        $base = $this->filteredQuery();

        [$this->statusFilter, $this->filters] = [$statusFilter, $filters];

        $totals = (clone $base)->reorder()->toBase()
            ->selectRaw('leads.lead_status_id as status_id, COUNT(*) as lead_count, COALESCE(SUM(leads.expected_value), 0) as value_total')
            ->groupBy('leads.lead_status_id')
            ->get()->keyBy('status_id');

        return LeadStatus::query()->active()->open()->ordered()->get()->map(fn (LeadStatus $status): array => [
            'id' => $status->id,
            'code' => $status->code,
            'name' => $status->name,
            'color' => $status->color,
            'count' => (int) ($totals[$status->id]->lead_count ?? 0),
            'total' => number_format((float) ($totals[$status->id]->value_total ?? 0), 2, '.', ''),
            'leads' => (clone $base)->where('leads.lead_status_id', $status->id)->limit(self::KANBAN_CARDS_PER_COLUMN)->get(),
        ])->all();
    }

    public function moveLead(string $leadId, int $position, string $statusId, ChangeLeadStatus $changeLeadStatus): void
    {
        $lead = Lead::query()->visibleTo($this->actor(), 'crm.leads')->find((int) $leadId);

        if ($lead === null || (int) $statusId === $lead->lead_status_id) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            $changeLeadStatus->handle($this->actor(), $lead, (int) $statusId);
        } catch (ValidationException $exception) {
            if (array_key_exists('follow_up', $exception->errors())) {
                $this->dispatch('crm-change-status', lead: $lead->lead_number, mode: 'status', status: (int) $statusId);

                return;
            }

            $this->dispatch('toast', type: 'error', description: (string) collect($exception->errors())->flatten()->first());
        } catch (AuthorizationException) {
            $this->dispatch('toast', type: 'error', description: __('You cannot change this lead.'));
        }
    }

    public function bulkAssign(AssignLead $assignLead): void
    {
        $this->authorize('crm.leads.assign');

        $this->runBulk(fn (Lead $lead) => $assignLead->handle($this->actor(), $lead, $this->bulkAssigneeId === '' ? null : (int) $this->bulkAssigneeId));
    }

    public function bulkChangeStatus(ChangeLeadStatus $changeLeadStatus): void
    {
        $this->authorize('crm.leads.update');

        $this->runBulk(fn (Lead $lead) => $changeLeadStatus->handle($this->actor(), $lead, (int) $this->bulkStatusId));
    }

    /**
     * Runs the change on every selected lead the user can see; failures are counted, not fatal.
     *
     * @param  \Closure(Lead): mixed  $change
     */
    private function runBulk(\Closure $change): void
    {
        $done = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        $leads = Lead::query()->visibleTo($this->actor(), 'crm.leads')->whereKey(array_map('intval', $this->selected))->get();
        $skipped += count($this->selected) - $leads->count();

        foreach ($leads as $lead) {
            try {
                $change($lead);
                $done++;
            } catch (ValidationException|AuthorizationException) {
                $skipped++;
            }
        }

        $this->reset('selected', 'bulkAssigneeId', 'bulkStatusId');
        $this->dispatch('close-sheet-lead-bulk');
        $this->dispatch('toast', type: $skipped > 0 ? 'warning' : 'success', description: __(':done updated, :skipped skipped.', ['done' => $done, 'skipped' => $skipped]));
    }

    public function export(): BinaryFileResponse
    {
        $this->authorize('crm.leads.export');

        return ListingExport::download('leads', $this->filteredQuery(), [
            'Lead #' => 'lead_number',
            'Date' => fn (Lead $lead): string => $lead->lead_date->format('d-M-Y'),
            'Name' => 'name',
            'Company' => 'company_name',
            'Phone' => 'phone',
            'Source' => 'source.name',
            'Business line' => 'businessLine.name',
            'Services' => fn (Lead $lead): string => $lead->services->pluck('service.name')->implode(', '),
            'Level' => 'level.name',
            'Status' => 'status.name',
            'Priority' => 'priority.name',
            'Assigned to' => 'assignee.name',
            'Team' => 'team.name',
            'Expected value' => 'expected_value',
            'Next follow-up' => fn (Lead $lead): ?string => $lead->next_follow_up_at?->format('d-M-Y H:i'),
            'Last activity' => fn (Lead $lead): ?string => $lead->last_activity_at?->format('d-M-Y H:i'),
        ]);
    }

    #[On('crm-lead-updated')]
    public function refreshList(): void {}

    public function staleDays(): int
    {
        return (int) Settings::get('crm.stale_lead_days', 14);
    }

    private function actor(): User
    {
        /** @var User */
        return auth()->user();
    }

    public function render(AssignLead $assignLead): View
    {
        return view('livewire.crm.leads.index', [
            'rows' => $this->view === 'list' ? $this->paginatedRows() : null,
            'mobileRows' => $this->mobileRows(),
            'columns' => $this->view === 'kanban' ? $this->kanbanColumns() : [],
            'statuses' => LeadStatus::query()->active()->ordered()->get(['id', 'name', 'code', 'is_closed']),
            'assignees' => $this->actor()->can('crm.leads.assign') ? $assignLead->assignableUsers($this->actor()) : collect(),
            'staleCutoff' => now()->subDays($this->staleDays()),
        ]);
    }
}
```

Import `Closure` and `Illuminate\Support\Collection` instead of the inline FQCNs. `crm-lead-updated` re-renders the list after the change-status sheet saves.

```php
<?php

beforeEach(function () {

function fillLeadForm($component, string $phone = '01711000000')
{

        ->set('name', 'Rahim Uddin')

test('routes need create and update rights; converted leads cannot be edited', function () {
    $mine = Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->user)->create();
    $converted = Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->user)->converted(Customer::factory()->create())->create();

        ->call('save')->assertHasErrors('duplicates')

        ->call('save')

test('service rows are added and removed and the expected value follows them until typed', function () {

    fillLeadForm(Livewire::actingAs($this->user)->test(Form::class))
        ->set('services.0.estimated_value', '100000')
        ->call('addService')
        ->set('services.1.service_id', (string) $other->id)
        ->set('services.1.estimated_value', '50000')
        ->assertSet('expected_value', '150000.00')
        ->set('expected_value', '200000')
        ->assertSet('expected_value_manual', true)
        ->call('removeService', 1)
        ->call('save')
        ->assertHasNoErrors();

    expect(Lead::query()->first())->expected_value->toBe('200000.00')->and(Lead::query()->first()->services()->count())->toBe(1);
});

test('editing keeps the services and saves changes', function () {
    $lead = Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->user)->create(['name' => 'Old Name']);
    $lead->services()->create(['service_id' => $this->service->id]);

    Livewire::actingAs($this->user)->test(Form::class, ['lead' => $lead])
        ->assertSet('services.0.service_id', $this->service->id)
        ->set('name', 'New Name')
        ->call('save')
        ->assertHasNoErrors()
        ->assertRedirect(route('crm.leads.show', $lead));

    expect($lead->fresh()->name)->toBe('New Name');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The component**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Leads/Form.php`:
- **Properties:** one per `CreateLead` field (ids as `int|string|null`, strings default `''`, `lead_date` default today), plus:
  - `services` (one empty row on create);
  - `whatsappSameAsPhone`, `expected_value_manual`;
  - `duplicates` (`list<array{type, id, number, name, status, owner}>`), `overrideDuplicates`, `duplicate_reason`;
  - `withFollowUp`, `follow_up_type_id`, `follow_up_at`, `referrerSearch`.
- **`mount(?Lead $lead)`.** Create: `authorize('create', Lead::class)`, default `lead_priority_id` = NORMAL id, `assigned_to` = the actor when they `can('crm.leads.view')`. Edit: `authorize('update', $lead)`, then fill fields and services from the model; `expected_value_manual` = true when the stored value differs from the services' sum.
- **Live updates:**
  - `updatedPhone()`, `updatedWhatsapp()`, `updatedOfficePhone()`, `updatedEmail()` → `checkDuplicates()`. It calls `FindDuplicates` with the current values (ignoring the edited lead) and maps each `DuplicateMatch` to an array. When `whatsappSameAsPhone`, `updatedPhone()` also copies `phone` to `whatsapp`.
  - `updatedWhatsappSameAsPhone(bool $value)` copies the phone when true.
  - `updatedServices()` and `updatedExpectedValue()`: the first recomputes `expected_value` from the rows (sum with two decimals; `''` when no row has a value) unless `expected_value_manual`; the second sets `expected_value_manual = true`.
  - `updatedLeadSourceId()` clears the referrer fields when the new source does not require one.
- **Actions:**
  - `addService()` / `removeService(int $index)`; at least one row stays.
  - `addAsEnquiry(int $customerId)`: the id must be one of the current customer matches. Sets the source to EXISTING, `referrer_type = 'customer'`, `referrer_id`, `referrer_name` = the customer name.
  - `save(CreateLead $createLead, UpdateLead $updateLead)`: builds the input array. `follow_up` only when `withFollowUp`. `duplicate_reason` only when `overrideDuplicates`. Calls the matching Action.
    - On a `ValidationException` whose errors include `duplicates`: refresh `duplicates` and rethrow.
    - On success: flash and `redirectRoute('crm.leads.show', $lead, navigate: true)`.
- **`render()`:**
  - `referrerCustomers` (when `referrer_type === 'customer'` and `referrerSearch` has 2+ characters: up to 8 visible customers matching number, name or phone);
  - `employees` (active users) and `assignees` (`AssignLead::assignableUsers()`);
  - `teams` (active, only when round-robin is on);
  - `requiresReferrer` (from the chosen source).
  - Title "New lead" / "Edit lead"; layout `back` → list (create) or show (edit), `bottomNav => false`.

- [ ] **Step 4: The view**

`resources/views/livewire/crm/leads/form.blade.php` (`x-shell.form-screen wire:submit="save"`):
- **`x-slot:alerts` — duplicate panel**, shown when `$duplicates` is not empty:
  - Desktop: an `x-ui.alert` titled "Possible duplicate". Mobile: the same content inside a bottom sheet `lead-duplicates` that opens automatically (`x-init` dispatching `open-sheet-lead-duplicates` when matches appear), plus an inline banner with a "Review" button.
  - One row per match: number, name, status, owner, and **Open existing** (link to the lead show page, or to customer edit/show).
  - For customer matches: **Add as new enquiry for this customer** (`wire:click="addAsEnquiry({{ $match['id'] }})"`).
  - Then a **Create anyway** switch (`overrideDuplicates`) revealing the Reason input (`duplicate_reason`).
  - `x-ui.field-error` for `duplicates`.
- **Contact:**
  - Name*, Company;
  - Phone* (`type="tel"`, `wire:model.live.debounce.500ms`);
  - WhatsApp with a "Same as phone" checkbox (disables the input when checked);
  - Office phone, Email (`type="email"`, live debounced);
  - Address (textarea), Location (`x-location-select`).
- **Enquiry:**
  - Lead date* (`type="date"`, `max` today), Source* (`x-lookup-select table="lead_sources" wire:model.live`).
  - When `$requiresReferrer`: Referrer type (native select: Customer, Employee, Agent, Other). For Customer: a search input (`referrerSearch`, live) and a result list of `x-ui.item` rows (44 px) to pick from, showing the picked one with a clear button. Employee: native select of `$employees`. Agent / Other: Referrer name. Errors for `referrer_name` and `referrer_id`.
  - Business line, **Services*** (repeater rows: service native select of active services, estimated value `inputmode="decimal"`, note, remove button; "Add service"; errors per row and `services`).
  - Level, Priority*, Expected value (`inputmode="decimal"`, `wire:model.live.debounce.500ms`, description "Filled from the services until you type a value"), Expected close date.
  - Site location, Land area, Floors planned (`inputmode="numeric"`), Notes.
- **Assignment** (create only):
  - Assigned to (native select of `$assignees`, with "Unassigned");

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `Customer`:

```php
    /**
     * Activities on the customer and on its converted and referred leads (spec R17).
     *
     * @return Builder<CrmActivity>
     */
    public function timeline(): Builder
    {
        $leadIds = Lead::query()->select('id')
            ->where('converted_customer_id', $this->id)
            ->orWhere(fn (Builder $query) => $query->where('referrer_type', 'customer')->where('referrer_id', $this->id));

        return CrmActivity::query()->where(fn (Builder $query) => $query
            ->where(fn (Builder $query) => $query->where('subject_type', $this->getMorphClass())->where('subject_id', $this->id))
            ->orWhere(fn (Builder $query) => $query->where('subject_type', (new Lead)->getMorphClass())->whereIn('subject_id', $leadIds)));
    }
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - **`ConvertLead::handle(User $actor, Lead $lead, array $input): Customer`.** Input is either `customer_id` (link) or `customer` (an array of `SaveCustomer` input).
    - Linking needs a live, non-blocked customer.
    - Creating passes the attribution `source_lead_id`, `lead_source_id` and `acquired_by_user_id` (the lead's assignee, else the actor).
    - Linking fills attribution only where the customer has none (CRM-BR-16).
    - Sets WON, `won_at`, `converted_customer_id`, `converted_at` and `converted_by`, and writes history. Dispatches `LeadConverted` inside the transaction, so a failing listener rolls everything back.
    - Notifies `LeadWon` to the team manager and every active `management` user (spec R14).
  - **`LeadConverted`** event (`public Lead $lead, public Customer $customer`). 04 adds the project step and a listener.
  - Route `crm.leads.convert` (`can:convert,lead`).
  - The change-status sheet shows "Won → convert" (with `crm.leads.convert`), which redirects to the wizard.

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Events;

use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Customer;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Events\Dispatchable;

/**
 * A lead was won and converted (docs/03 §8). Dispatched inside the conversion transaction, so a
 * listener that throws (04's project creation) rolls the whole conversion back (CRM-AC-06).
 */

{

    public function __construct(public Lead $lead, public Customer $customer) {}
}
```

`app/Modules/Crm/Actions/ConvertLead.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Actions;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Crm\Events\LeadConverted;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Customer;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\LeadStatus;
use App\Modules\Crm\Notifications\LeadWon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

/**
 * Converts a won lead into a customer (docs/03 §5.7, CRM-BR-09). The project step arrives with
 * 04 (spec R1); until then conversion is customer + lead update in one transaction.
 */
class ConvertLead
{
    public function __construct(private SaveCustomer $saveCustomer) {}

    /**
     * @param  array{customer_id?: int|string|null, customer?: array<string, mixed>}  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function handle(User $actor, Lead $lead, array $input): Customer
    {
        Gate::forUser($actor)->authorize('convert', $lead);
        LeadState::ensureOpen($lead);

        $linked = $this->linkedCustomer($input);
        $attribution = [
            'source_lead_id' => $lead->id,
            'lead_source_id' => $lead->lead_source_id,
            'acquired_by_user_id' => $lead->assigned_to ?? $actor->id,
        ];

        $customer = DB::transaction(function () use ($actor, $lead, $input, $linked, $attribution): Customer {
            if ($linked !== null) {
                $customer = $linked;

                foreach ($attribution as $column => $value) {
                    $customer->{$column} ??= $value;
                }

                $customer->save();
            } else {
                $customer = $this->saveCustomer->handle($actor, $input['customer'] ?? [], null, $attribution);
            }

            LeadState::recordStatus($actor, $lead, LeadStatus::idFor(LeadStatus::WON), __('Converted to :number', ['number' => $customer->customer_number]), [
                'won_at' => now(),
                'converted_customer_id' => $customer->id,
                'converted_at' => now(),
                'converted_by' => $actor->id,
            ]);

            LeadConverted::dispatch($lead, $customer);

            return $customer;
        });

        Notification::send($this->wonRecipients($lead), new LeadWon($lead, $customer));

        return $customer;
    }

    /**
     * @param  array{customer_id?: int|string|null}  $input
     *
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    private function linkedCustomer(array $input): ?Customer
    {
        if (empty($input['customer_id'])) {
            return null;
        }

        $customer = Customer::query()->whereNull('merged_into_id')->with('status')->find((int) $input['customer_id']);

        if ($customer === null || $customer->isBlocked()) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['customer_id' => __('Choose an active customer that is not blocked.')]);
        }

        return $customer;
    }

    /**
     * The lead's team manager and every active management user (spec R14).
     *
     * @return \Illuminate\Support\Collection<int, User>
     */
    private function wonRecipients(Lead $lead): \Illuminate\Support\Collection
    {
        $managerId = $lead->team?->manager_user_id;

        return User::query()->where('is_active', true)
            ->where(fn ($query) => $query->whereHas('roles', fn ($query) => $query->where('code', 'management'))
                ->when($managerId !== null, fn ($query) => $query->orWhere('id', $managerId)))
            ->get();
    }
}
```

Import `Illuminate\Support\Collection`. `Customer`'s attribution columns are not fillable, so the `??=` assignments use attribute access (allowed; mass assignment is what `$fillable` guards).

- [ ] **Step 4: The wizard**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Leads/Convert.php`:
- `public Lead $lead`, `public int $step = 1`, `public string $choice = 'link'` (`link` | `new`), `public int|string|null $customerId`, `public string $search = ''`, `public array $customer = []` (the `SaveCustomer` fields), `public string $duplicate_reason = ''`.
- `mount(Lead $lead)`:
  - `authorize('convert', $lead)`.
  - The suggested customers are customer matches from `FindDuplicates` on the lead's phone, WhatsApp, office phone and email, plus the lead's `referrerCustomer` when `referrer_type = customer`.
  - Pre-select that referrer when present, otherwise the first suggestion; with no suggestion, set `choice = 'new'`.
  - Prefill `customer` from the lead: name, company_name, phone, whatsapp, alternate_phone ← office_phone, email, address, location_id, business_line_id, account_manager_user_id ← assignee, `customer_type_id` ← COMPANY when `company_name` is set else INDIVIDUAL, `contacts => []`.
- `render()` adds search results when `search` has 2+ characters: up to 10 non-merged customers by number, name or phone. Linking does not need the customer to be in the user's own scope, because duplicates are shown the same way.
- `next()`:
  - `link`: validate that `customerId` is set (`customerId` error otherwise);
  - `new`: validate only the required customer fields here (`customer.customer_type_id`, `customer.name`, `customer.phone`) with `$this->validate([...])`; the final `convert()` reports anything else from `SaveCustomer`.
  - Then `step = 2`.
- `back()` → `step = 1`.
- `convert(ConvertLead $convertLead)`: call with `['customer_id' => ...]` or `['customer' => [...$this->customer, 'duplicate_reason' => $this->duplicate_reason ?: null]]`. Validation errors from `customer.*` come back without the prefix: re-throw them prefixed with `customer.` so they show on the fields (same pattern as `Admin\Settings::save()`). If an error lands on step-1 fields, set `step = 1`. On success, flash and redirect to `crm.customers.show`.

`resources/views/livewire/crm/leads/convert.blade.php`:
- **Desktop:** an `x-ui.stepper` header (1 Customer, 2 Review & confirm). **Mobile:** full screen with the sticky bottom bar (Back / Next or Convert) from `x-shell.form-screen` — use `x-shell.form-screen wire:submit="{{ $step === 1 ? 'next' : 'convert' }}"` with `:submit-label` switching between "Next" and "Convert".
- **Step 1:**
  - A choice between "Link an existing customer" and "Create a new customer" (`x-ui.radio-group`, 44 px rows).
  - **Link:** suggested matches as selectable `x-ui.item` rows (number, name, phone, status badge; blocked ones disabled with a "Blocked" badge), a search input, and the search results.
  - **New:** the customer fields (Type*, Name*, Company, Phone*, WhatsApp, Alternate phone, Email, Address, Location, NID / reg. no., Business line, Account manager; with `crm.customers.update_finance` also TIN and Payment term), and the duplicate reason when that error appears.
- **Step 2:** a summary card — lead (number, name, services, expected value) → customer (linked one or the new one's fields). A note: "The lead becomes Won. Projects will be added here once the Projects module is live." Errors from the Action (`lead`, `customer_id`) are shown at the top.

Route (before the show route):

```php
    Route::livewire('leads/{lead:lead_number}/convert', Leads\Convert::class)->middleware('can:convert,lead')->name('leads.convert');
```

  - In `save()`, before the `match`: if `$this->mode === 'status' && $this->statusId === 'won'`, authorize `convert` and `redirectRoute('crm.leads.convert', $lead)`, then return.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Activities/Index.php`:
- **Base query:**

```php
    /**
     * @return Builder<CrmActivity>
     */
    private function scoped(): Builder
    {
        $query = CrmActivity::query()
            ->visibleTo($this->actor(), 'crm.activities')
            ->with(['type', 'outcome', 'owner:id,name', 'subject' => fn (MorphTo $morphTo) => $morphTo->morphWith([Lead::class => [], Customer::class => []])]);

        return match ($this->owner) {
            'all' => $query,
            'me' => $query->where('owner_user_id', $this->actor()->id),
            default => $query->where('owner_user_id', (int) $this->owner),
        };
    }
```

  `visibleTo` already limits the owner to the user's scope, so an out-of-scope owner id returns nothing beyond what the scope allows. The test above expects the user's own rows to still show: when the requested owner is not in the scope (`! in_array((int) $this->owner, [$me, ...SalesTeam::managedMemberIds($me)])` without `view_all`), reset `owner` to `me` in `updatedOwner()` and in `mount()`.
- **Tabs:**
  - `overdue` = open + `scheduled_at < now()`, oldest first;
  - `today` = open + `scheduled_at` between now and end of today, soonest first;
  - `upcoming` = open + `scheduled_at > end of today`;
  - `done` = `completed_at` not null, latest first, `WithPagination` (25).
  - Also pass counts per tab for the tab badges.
- **Calendar:**
  - `weekStart()` = `Carbon::parse($this->date)->startOfWeek(Carbon::SATURDAY)`.
  - `events` = open and done activities with `scheduled_at` (or, for done ones without it, `completed_at`) inside the week, mapped to the `x-ui.scheduler` shape `['title' => …, 'day' => 0..6, 'start' => 'H:i', 'end' => 'H:i' (start + duration or + 30 min), 'color' => …]`.
  - `days` labels = the seven dates (`D d M`).
- `deleteActivity(DeleteActivity)` with a confirmation sheet, as on the lead page.
- `#[On('crm-activity-saved')]` re-renders.

`resources/views/livewire/crm/activities/index.blade.php`:
- **Header:**
  - Desktop: tabs with count badges, an owner select (only with `view_team` / `view_all`: Me, All, then the people in scope) and a List / Calendar segmented control.
  - Mobile: a segmented control for the tabs and an owner filter in a bottom sheet.
- **List:** the timeline partial with `showSubject` true (subject name linking to the lead or customer page). Mark done and Reschedule open the quick-log sheet in `complete` / `reschedule` mode.
- **Calendar:**
  - Desktop: week navigation (previous / this week / next) and `<x-ui.scheduler :events="$events" :days="$days" :start-hour="8" :end-hour="20" />`.
  - Mobile: a day-grouped agenda for the same week (`x-ui.item` rows under a sticky date heading), since a 7-column grid is not usable at 390 px.
- `<livewire:crm.quick-log />` and the `activity-delete` sheet.

Route:

```php
    Route::livewire('activities', Activities\Index::class)->middleware('can:crm.activities.view')->name('activities.index');
```

**Files:**

- Create: `app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Customers/Merge.php`, `resources/views/livewire/crm/customers/merge.blade.php`
- Modify: `routes/modules/crm.php`, `resources/views/livewire/crm/customers/show.blade.php` (Merge action)
- Test: `tests/Feature/Crm/MergeCustomersTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - **`MergeCustomers::preview(Customer $duplicate): array<string, int>`** — counts for `leads`, `referred_leads`, `activities`, `contacts`, `attachments` and `notes`.
  - **`MergeCustomers::handle(User $actor, Customer $survivor, Customer $duplicate, string $reason): Customer`.**
    - Needs `merge` on both customers and two different, non-merged customers. The reason is required.
    - In one transaction it moves the rows counted by the preview. Moved contacts lose `is_primary` when the survivor already has a primary.
    - It fills the survivor's empty attribution from the duplicate (the earlier `source_lead_id` wins when both have one) and writes one `merged` audit row per table on the survivor (`table`, `count`, `from`, `reason`).
    - It dispatches `CustomersMerging(survivor, duplicate)`, sets `merged_into_id` and soft-deletes the duplicate.
  - Route `crm.customers.merge` (`can:merge,customer`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`app/Modules/Crm/Events/CustomersMerging.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Events;

use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Customer;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Events\Dispatchable;

/**
 * Two customers are being merged (docs/03 §5.12, spec R16). Dispatched inside the merge
 * transaction so 04, 06 and 08 can move projects, invoices, receipts and journal lines.
 */
class CustomersMerging
{
    use Dispatchable;

    public function __construct(public Customer $survivor, public Customer $duplicate) {}
}
```

`app/Modules/Crm/Actions/MergeCustomers.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Actions;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Crm\Events\CustomersMerging;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\CrmActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Customer;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use App\Modules\Foundation\Models\Attachment;
use App\Modules\Foundation\Models\Note;
use App\Support\AuditTrail\AuditTrail;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Gate;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

/**
 * Merges a duplicate customer into a survivor (docs/03 §5.12, spec R16).
 */
class MergeCustomers
{
    /**
     * @return array<string, int>
     */
    public function preview(Customer $duplicate): array
    {
        return array_map(fn (Builder $query): int => $query->count(), $this->queries($duplicate));
    }

    /**
     * @throws ValidationException
     */
    public function handle(User $actor, Customer $survivor, Customer $duplicate, string $reason): Customer
    {
        Gate::forUser($actor)->authorize('merge', $survivor);
        Gate::forUser($actor)->authorize('merge', $duplicate);

        if (trim($reason) === '') {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['reason' => __('Give a reason for the merge.')]);
        }

        if ($survivor->is($duplicate) || $survivor->merged_into_id !== null || $duplicate->merged_into_id !== null) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['duplicate' => __('Choose two different customers that are not merged already.')]);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($actor, $survivor, $duplicate, $reason): Customer {
            $survivorHasPrimary = $survivor->contacts()->where('is_primary', true)->exists();
            $moves = [
                'leads' => ['converted_customer_id' => $survivor->id],
                'referred_leads' => ['referrer_id' => $survivor->id],
                'activities' => ['subject_id' => $survivor->id],
                'contacts' => ['customer_id' => $survivor->id, ...($survivorHasPrimary ? ['is_primary' => false] : [])],
                'attachments' => ['attachable_id' => $survivor->id],
                'notes' => ['notable_id' => $survivor->id],
            ];

            foreach ($this->queries($duplicate) as $table => $query) {
                $count = $query->toBase()->update($moves[$table]);

                if ($count > 0) {
                    AuditTrail::record($survivor, 'merged', null, ['table' => $table, 'count' => $count, 'from' => $duplicate->customer_number, 'reason' => $reason], $actor);
                }
            }

            $this->carryAttribution($survivor, $duplicate);

            CustomersMerging::dispatch($survivor, $duplicate);

            $duplicate->forceFill(['merged_into_id' => $survivor->id])->save();
            $duplicate->delete();

            return $survivor->refresh();
        });
    }

    /**
     * Rows that point at the duplicate, keyed like preview().
     *
     * @return array<string, Builder<covariant \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model>>
     */
    private function queries(Customer $duplicate): array
    {
        $alias = $duplicate->getMorphClass();

        return [
            'leads' => Lead::withTrashed()->where('converted_customer_id', $duplicate->id),
            'referred_leads' => Lead::withTrashed()->where('referrer_type', 'customer')->where('referrer_id', $duplicate->id),
            'activities' => CrmActivity::withTrashed()->where('subject_type', $alias)->where('subject_id', $duplicate->id),
            'contacts' => $duplicate->contacts()->withTrashed()->getQuery(),
            'attachments' => Attachment::withTrashed()->where('attachable_type', $alias)->where('attachable_id', $duplicate->id),
            'notes' => Note::withTrashed()->where('notable_type', $alias)->where('notable_id', $duplicate->id),
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Keeps first-lead attribution (CRM-BR-16): the earlier source lead wins.
     */
    private function carryAttribution(Customer $survivor, Customer $duplicate): void
    {
        $useDuplicate = $duplicate->source_lead_id !== null
            && ($survivor->source_lead_id === null || $duplicate->source_lead_id < $survivor->source_lead_id);

        if ($useDuplicate) {
            $survivor->forceFill([
                'source_lead_id' => $duplicate->source_lead_id,
                'lead_source_id' => $duplicate->lead_source_id,
                'acquired_by_user_id' => $duplicate->acquired_by_user_id,
            ])->save();
        }
    }
}
```

The preview runs `count()` on the same queries. `withTrashed()` works on all of them: `Lead`, `CrmActivity`, `CustomerContact`, `Attachment` and `Note` all use `SoftDeletes`, so soft-deleted rows move with the customer too.

`app/Modules/Crm/Livewire/Customers/Merge.php`:
- `#[Locked] public int $survivorId`, `public string $duplicateId = ''`, `public string $search = ''`, `public string $reason = ''`, and a computed `preview` (array, empty until a duplicate is picked).
- `mount(Customer $customer)` authorizes `merge` and sets `survivorId`.
- `swap()` exchanges the survivor and the duplicate. Both stay authorized: re-check `merge` on the new survivor. `survivorId` is locked, so `swap()` is the only way to change it.
- `merge(MergeCustomers $merge)` → flash, redirect to the survivor.
- `render()` passes search results (non-merged customers other than the survivor matching number, name or phone, up to 10), both customers' summaries and the preview.
- `preview` is a public array refreshed in `updatedDuplicateId()` (the test reads `preview.leads`).

View: two summary cards side by side on desktop, stacked on mobile ("Keep" and "Merge into it"), with a Swap button between them. Then the preview table (what moves: n leads, n activities, …), a Reason input and a destructive "Merge customers" button; on mobile it sits in the sticky bottom bar. The page explains that the duplicate will be hidden and its number will stop working.

Route (after `customers/{customer}/edit`):

```php
    Route::livewire('customers/{customer:customer_number}/merge', Customers\Merge::class)->middleware('can:merge,customer')->name('customers.merge');
```

### Task 18: Reminders, daily digest and stale leads (docs/03 §6.2, §9, spec R13, R14)

**Files:**
- Create: `app/Modules/Crm/Notifications/{FollowUpReminder,DailyDigest,LeadStale}.php`
- Create: `app/Modules/Crm/Jobs/{SendDueReminders,SendDailyDigest,FlagStaleLeads}.php`
- Modify: `routes/console.php`
- Test: `tests/Feature/Crm/CrmJobsTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - **`SendDueReminders`** (queued job, every five minutes). Open activities with `reminder_at <= now()` and no `reminder_sent_at`. Each row is claimed with a conditional update (`whereNull('reminder_sent_at')->update(...)` returning 1) before `FollowUpReminder` goes to the owner, so a second run, or two overlapping runs, send nothing more.
  - **`SendDailyDigest`** (queued job, every minute). It acts once per day after `notifications.daily_digest_time` (`Cache::add('crm:daily-digest:'.today()->toDateString(), …)`). Every active user who `can('crm.activities.view')` and has open activities scheduled up to the end of today gets `DailyDigest` with their today and overdue items. A user who manages teams also gets their members' overdue counts, even with no items of their own.
  - **`FlagStaleLeads`** (queued job, daily at 08:00). Stale leads (`Lead::stale()`) with no `stale_notified_at` notify the assignee and the team manager with `LeadStale`, then set `stale_notified_at`.
  - Notifications: `FollowUpReminder` (`crm.follow_up_reminder`, mail + database), `DailyDigest` (`crm.daily_digest`, mail), `LeadStale` (`crm.lead_stale`, database).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`tests/Feature/Crm/CrmJobsTest.php`:

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use App\Modules\Crm\Jobs\FlagStaleLeads;
use App\Modules\Crm\Jobs\SendDailyDigest;
use App\Modules\Crm\Jobs\SendDueReminders;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\CrmActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\Lead;
use App\Modules\Crm\Models\SalesTeam;
use App\Modules\Crm\Notifications\DailyDigest;
use App\Modules\Crm\Notifications\FollowUpReminder;
use App\Modules\Crm\Notifications\LeadStale;
use App\Support\Facades\Settings;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;

beforeEach(function () {
    seedCrm();
    Notification::fake();
    $this->travelTo(now()->setDateTime(2026, 10, 7, 14, 0));
    $this->owner = userWithPermissions('crm.leads.view_own', 'crm.activities.view_own');
    $this->lead = Lead::factory()->assignedTo($this->owner)->create();
});

test('a 15:00 follow-up with a 30 minute reminder notifies at 14:30, once (CRM-AC-07)', function () {
    CrmActivity::factory()->on($this->lead)->create([
        'owner_user_id' => $this->owner->id, 'scheduled_at' => now()->setTime(15, 0), 'reminder_at' => now()->setTime(14, 30),
    ]);

    $this->travelTo(now()->setTime(14, 25));
    (new SendDueReminders)->handle();
    Notification::assertNothingSent();

    $this->travelTo(now()->setTime(14, 30));
    (new SendDueReminders)->handle();
    (new SendDueReminders)->handle();

    Notification::assertSentToTimes($this->owner, FollowUpReminder::class, 1);
});

test('completed activities get no reminder', function () {
    CrmActivity::factory()->on($this->lead)->done()->create(['owner_user_id' => $this->owner->id, 'reminder_at' => now()->subMinute()]);

    (new SendDueReminders)->handle();

    Notification::assertNothingSent();
});

test('the digest goes out once a day after the set time, with a team summary for managers', function () {
    Settings::set('notifications.daily_digest_time', '09:00');
    $manager = userWithPermissions('crm.leads.view_team', 'crm.activities.view_team');
    SalesTeam::factory()->managedBy($manager)->withMembers($this->owner)->create();

    CrmActivity::factory()->on($this->lead)->create(['owner_user_id' => $this->owner->id, 'scheduled_at' => now()->subDay()]);
    CrmActivity::factory()->on($this->lead)->create(['owner_user_id' => $this->owner->id, 'scheduled_at' => now()->addHours(2)]);

    $this->travelTo(now()->setTime(8, 59));
    (new SendDailyDigest)->handle();
    Notification::assertNothingSent();

    $this->travelTo(now()->setTime(9, 0));
    (new SendDailyDigest)->handle();
    (new SendDailyDigest)->handle();

    Notification::assertSentToTimes($this->owner, DailyDigest::class, 1);
    Notification::assertSentTo($this->owner, DailyDigest::class, fn (DailyDigest $digest) => $digest->overdue->count() === 1 && $digest->today->count() === 1);
    Notification::assertSentTo($manager, DailyDigest::class, fn (DailyDigest $digest) => $digest->teamOverdue === [$this->owner->name => 1]);
});

test('stale leads notify the owner and team manager once, and a new activity resets the flag', function () {
    $manager = User::factory()->create();
    $team = SalesTeam::factory()->managedBy($manager)->withMembers($this->owner)->create();
    $this->lead->forceFill(['sales_team_id' => $team->id])->save();

    $this->travel(15)->days();
    (new FlagStaleLeads)->handle();
    (new FlagStaleLeads)->handle();

    Notification::assertSentToTimes($this->owner, LeadStale::class, 1);
    Notification::assertSentToTimes($manager, LeadStale::class, 1);
    expect($this->lead->fresh()->stale_notified_at)->not->toBeNull();
});

test('the jobs are scheduled', function () {
    $this->artisan('schedule:list')->expectsOutputToContain('crm:reminders')->expectsOutputToContain('crm:daily-digest')->expectsOutputToContain('crm:stale-leads');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Notifications**

- `FollowUpReminder(CrmActivity $activity)` — `use SerializesModels;`, key `crm.follow_up_reminder`.
  - Mail subject: "Reminder: :title at :time".
  - Body: the subject's name and number, and an action linking to the lead or customer page.
  - `toArray`: title, body (type, time) and the url (`route('crm.leads.show', $subject)` or `route('crm.customers.show', $subject)` by `subject_type`).
- `DailyDigest`:
  - Public properties `Collection $today`, `Collection $overdue` (activities with `subject` and `type` loaded) and `array $teamOverdue` (member name → count).
  - Key `crm.daily_digest`, mail only.
  - Mail: subject "Your follow-ups for :date"; lines "n due today, m overdue"; up to 10 items each ("09:30 Call — Rahim Uddin (L-000124)"); for managers, a "Team overdue" list; an action to `crm.activities.index`.
- `LeadStale(Lead $lead, int $days)` — key `crm.lead_stale`, database only; `toArray` title "Lead :number has gone quiet", body "No activity for :days days.", url = the lead page.

- [ ] **Step 4: Jobs**

`app/Modules/Crm/Jobs/SendDueReminders.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Modules\Crm\Jobs;

use App\Modules\Crm\Models\CrmActivity;
use App\Modules\Crm\Notifications\FollowUpReminder;
use Illuminate\Bus\Queueable;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\ShouldQueue;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Bus\Dispatchable;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;

/**
 * Sends follow-up reminders that are due (docs/03 §6.2 step 2). Each row is claimed with a
 * conditional update first, so overlapping or repeated runs never send twice.
 */
class SendDueReminders implements ShouldQueue
{
    use Dispatchable, Queueable;

    public function handle(): void
    {
        CrmActivity::query()
            ->whereNull('completed_at')
            ->whereNotNull('reminder_at')
            ->whereNull('reminder_sent_at')
            ->where('reminder_at', '<=', now())
            ->with(['owner', 'subject', 'type'])
            ->chunkById(100, function (Collection $activities): void {
                foreach ($activities as $activity) {
                    $claimed = CrmActivity::query()->whereKey($activity->id)->whereNull('reminder_sent_at')->update(['reminder_sent_at' => now()]);

                    if ($claimed === 1 && $activity->owner->is_active) {
                        $activity->owner->notify(new FollowUpReminder($activity));
                    }
                }
            });
    }
}
```

`SendDailyDigest::handle()`:

```php
        $time = (string) Settings::get('notifications.daily_digest_time', '09:00');

        if (now()->format('H:i') < $time || ! Cache::add('crm:daily-digest:'.today()->toDateString(), true, now()->endOfDay())) {
            return;
        }

        User::query()->where('is_active', true)->each(function (User $user): void {
            if (! $user->can('crm.activities.view')) {
                return;
            }

            $open = CrmActivity::query()->where('owner_user_id', $user->id)->whereNull('completed_at')
                ->whereNotNull('scheduled_at')->where('scheduled_at', '<=', today()->endOfDay())
                ->with(['subject', 'type'])->orderBy('scheduled_at')->get();

            [$overdue, $today] = $open->partition(fn (CrmActivity $activity): bool => $activity->scheduled_at->lt(today()->startOfDay()) || $activity->scheduled_at->isPast());

            $teamOverdue = $this->teamOverdue($user);

            if ($open->isEmpty() && $teamOverdue === []) {
                return;
            }

            $user->notify(new DailyDigest($today->values(), $overdue->values(), $teamOverdue));
        });
```

with `teamOverdue(User $manager): array<string, int>` — for each active member id from `SalesTeam::managedMemberIds($manager)` (excluding the manager), the count of their open activities with `scheduled_at < now()`, keyed by name, members with zero left out.

The partition puts an activity scheduled earlier today but already past into "overdue", matching the My activities tabs. In the test, the item at `now()->addHours(2)` lands in `today`, and the one from yesterday in `overdue`.

`FlagStaleLeads::handle()`:

```php
        $days = (int) Settings::get('crm.stale_lead_days', 14);

        Lead::query()->stale($days)->whereNull('stale_notified_at')->with(['assignee', 'team.manager'])
            ->chunkById(100, function (Collection $leads) use ($days): void {
                foreach ($leads as $lead) {
                    $recipients = collect([$lead->assignee, $lead->team?->manager])->filter(fn (?User $user): bool => $user?->is_active === true)->unique('id');

                    $lead->forceFill(['stale_notified_at' => now()])->saveQuietly();

                    Notification::send($recipients, new LeadStale($lead, $days));
                }
            });
```

`routes/console.php`:

```php
Schedule::job(new SendDueReminders)->everyFiveMinutes()->name('crm:reminders')->withoutOverlapping();
Schedule::job(new SendDailyDigest)->everyMinute()->name('crm:daily-digest')->withoutOverlapping();
Schedule::job(new FlagStaleLeads)->dailyAt('08:00')->name('crm:stale-leads')->withoutOverlapping();
```
